I Had A Dream
i had an experience of being like “well i’m so ugly, i’m not allowed to like cute things esp bc of the way i’ve been defeminized, i bet ppl would find it gross/weird etc” but honestly… at some point i stopped caring LOL. i feel like life is hard enough as an ugly woman as it is without feeling like i Have to appease the expectations of the people who make my life miserable. it doesn’t suit me? whatever. nothing suits me anyway. it’s kind of liberating in a way. i just hate the thought of seeing the ugly world and being treated like shit and then coming home and continuing to feel like it’s still there. i come back to my room and i see plushies and cute things that make me happy and i get a respite from all that. i think everyone deserves that, so why not me? for that matter, i think you deserve that too, so why not you?

is anything even worth it at all ? like every day i just wake up, eat my breakfast then i sit on the pc and stare at my screen, atp i dont even do anything "productive" anymore, i remember back when i was a teenager i could sit there for hours on end and atleast talk to friends and play a game or i could sit down and read but now my attention is so raped that i can only stare at the screen and watch youtube videos or whatever i cant even watch movies? sometimes i do play games but it just makes me unhappy or mad or like sad that im not as good at the games. i try drawing or making music but those suck as well i always compare myself to others and feel like im worthless and amount to nothing. my job also mainly consists of doing stuff on the computer and doesnt have any real value like someone who builds something or whatever. it just feels so disgusting my entire life and i hate it but i dont know how to break free from it. i look in the mirror and see someone i dont like, i dont recognise the girl in th(...)
